{"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"05c7889daca9abfdb0f9c2bb16bb6b98","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Perfect! I found it! Lines 55-61 show the problematic dependency:\n\n```xml\n<dependency>\n  <groupId>com.sun</groupId>\n  <artifactId>tools</artifactId>\n  <version>${java.version}</version>\n  <scope>system</scope>\n  <systemPath>${toolsjar}</systemPath>\n</dependency>\n```\n\nThis is a system-scoped dependency on `com.sun:tools` that references `${toolsjar}`. The `toolsjar` property is defined in the profiles (lines 106 and 118).\n\nThe problem is that in JDK 9+, the `tools.jar` no longer exists because the JDK modules were reorganized.
